Abstract: The deployment of Large Language Models (LLMs) for specialized engineering domains, such as circuit analysis, often faces a trade-off between reasoning accuracy and computational efficiency. Traditional evaluation methods treat model performance as a flat metric, failing to account for the hierarchical nature of engineering knowledge. We propose a performance-aware model compression strategy that utilizes prerequisite graphs to optimize model selection for circuit analysis tasks. By structuring electronics design concepts as Directed Acyclic Graphs (DAGs), we can identify the specific complexity horizons of an LLM's compressed variants' tiers. Our framework introduces an agentic pipeline for generating prerequisite-based datasets and a strategic evaluation engine that dynamically cascades queries across a spectrum of compressed variants of an LLM. This approach allows to select the smallest compressed model, given its conceptual knowledge boundaries in circuit analysis. Experimental results on analog electronics datasets demonstrate that prerequisite graphs provide a granular map of model compression with respect to the performance given circuit analysis complexity. (Source Code: https://github.com/pacomesimon/LLM_prereq_graphs_circuit_analysis, Demo: https://huggingface.co/spaces/pacomesimon/LLM_prereq_graphs_circuit_analysis)

</details>


### [148] [EngiAgent: Fully Connected Coordination of LLM Agents for Solving Open-ended Engineering Problems with Feasible Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02289)
*Xiyuan Zhou,Ruixi Zou,Xinlei Wang,Yuheng Cheng,Yan Xu,Junhua Zhao,Jinjin Gu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EngiAgent是一个多智能体系统，通过全连接协调器模拟专家工作流程，在工程问题求解中显著提升可行性，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLMs虽然在推理和代码生成方面表现出色，但在工程问题求解中常常无法保证解的可行性，限制了其在真实工程决策中的应用。

Method: 提出EngiAgent，一种多智能体系统，包含问题分析、建模、验证、求解和方案评估等专业智能体，并通过全连接协调器实现灵活的反馈路由，取代传统的流水线式反思方法。

Result: 在四个代表性领域的实验表明，EngiAgent在可行性方面相较于现有方法取得了显著提升。

Conclusion: EngiAgent为基于LLMs的可行性导向工程问题求解建立了新范式，代码和数据已开源。

Abstract: Engineering problem solving is central to real-world decision-making, requiring mathematical formulations that not only represent complex problems but also produce feasible solutions under data and physical constraints. Unlike mathematical problem solving, which operates on predefined formulations, engineering tasks demand open-ended analysis, feasibility-driven modeling, and iterative refinement. Although large language models (LLMs) have shown strong capabilities in reasoning and code generation, they often fail to ensure feasibility, which limits their applicability to engineering problem solving. To address this challenge, we propose EngiAgent, a multi-agent system with a fully connected coordinator that simulates expert workflows through specialized agents for problem analysis, modeling, verification, solving, and solution evaluation. The fully connected coordinator enables flexible feedback routing, overcoming the rigidity of prior pipeline-based reflection methods and ensuring feasibility at every stage of the process. This design not only improves robustness to diverse failure cases such as data extraction errors, constraint inconsistencies, and solver failures, but also enhances the overall quality of problem solving. Empirical results across four representative domains demonstrate that EngiAgent achieves substantial improvements in feasibility compared to prior approaches, establishing a new paradigm for feasibility-oriented engineering problem solving with LLMs. Our source code and data are available at https://github.com/AI4Engi/EngiAgent.

</details>


### [149] [Distilling Long-CoT Reasoning through Collaborative Step-wise Multi-Teacher Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02290)
*Taewon Yun,Jisu Shin,Jeonghwan Choi,Seunghwan Bang,Hwanjun Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CoRD是一个协同多教师解码框架，通过逐步推理合成和基于困惑度的波束搜索，从多个大型推理模型中高效蒸馏高质量的长链推理数据，使学生模型在少量监督信号下达到接近教师水平的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蒸馏方法依赖事后选择完整推理轨迹，无法利用多个异构教师之间的协作，且缺乏动态探索，导致采样冗余并遗漏互补推理。

Method: 提出CoRD框架，采用协同多教师解码，通过预测性困惑度评分和波束搜索进行逐步推理合成，让异构大型推理模型共同构建连贯的推理轨迹，同时高效保留多样化、高潜力的推理假设。

Result: CoRD生成更高质量的推理数据，在更少、更结构化的监督信号下使学生性能接近教师水平，且无显著效率开销。在跨领域和开放式场景下泛化良好。

Conclusion: 协同多教师逐步解码蒸馏是比事后筛选更高效、更优质的推理数据合成策略。

Abstract: Distilling large reasoning models is essential for making Long-CoT reasoning practical, as full-scale inference remains computationally prohibitive. Existing curation-based approaches select complete reasoning traces post-hoc, overlooking collaboration among heterogeneous teachers and lacking dynamic exploration, which leads to redundant sampling and missed complementary reasoning. We introduce CoRD, a collaborative multi-teacher decoding framework that performs step-wise reasoning synthesis guided by predictive perplexity-based scoring and beam search. This enables heterogeneous LRMs to jointly construct coherent reasoning trajectories while efficiently preserving diverse, high-potential hypotheses. Experiments show that CoRD produces higher-quality reasoning data and achieves near teacher-level student performance with fewer, structured supervision signals, without substantial efficiency overhead. CoRD further generalizes well to out-of-domain and open-ended settings. The dataset and model are available at \href{https://github.com/DISL-Lab/CoRD}{https://github.com/DISL-Lab/CoRD}.

</details>


### [150] [Anon: Extrapolating Optimizer Adaptivity Across the Real Spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02317)
*Yiheng Zhang,Kaiyan Zhao,Shaowu Wu,Yiming Wang,Jiajun Wu,Leong Hou U,Steve Drew,Xiaoguang Niu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Anon优化器，通过连续可调的适配性机制（从SGD到Adam乃至更广）和增量延迟更新（IDU）策略，在理论上保证收敛，实验上在图像分类、扩散模型和语言建模任务中均优于现有优化器，首次实现了经典优化器与现代优化器的统一与超越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应优化器（如Adam）在大规模模型训练中成功，但泛化性能往往不如非自适应方法（如SGD），原因是预条件器的适配性限制了优化器适应多样化优化景观的能力。

Method: 提出Anon优化器，核心包含两个创新：1）连续可调的适配性机制，使优化器能在SGD-like和Adam-like行为之间插值甚至外推；2）增量延迟更新（IDU），一种比AMSGrad更灵活的机制，增强对梯度噪声的鲁棒性。

Result: 在图像分类、扩散模型和语言建模任务中，Anon一致性地优于现有最先进的优化器。

Conclusion: 适配性可以作为有价值且可调的设计原则，Anon提供了首个统一且可靠的框架，能够弥合经典优化器与现代优化器之间的差距并超越它们的优势特性。

Abstract: Adaptive optimizers such as Adam have achieved great success in training large-scale models like large language models and diffusion models. However, they often generalize worse than non-adaptive methods, such as SGD on classical architectures like CNNs. We identify a key cause of this performance gap: adaptivity in pre-conditioners, which limits the optimizer's ability to adapt to diverse optimization landscapes. To address this, we propose Anon (Adaptivity Non-restricted Optimizer with Novel convergence technique), a novel optimizer with continuously tunable adaptivity in R, allowing it to interpolate between SGD-like and Adam-like behaviors and even extrapolate beyond both. To ensure convergence across the entire adaptivity spectrum, we introduce incremental delay update (IDU), a novel mechanism that is more flexible than AMSGrad's hard max-tracking strategy and enhances robustness to gradient noise. We theoretically establish convergence guarantees under both convex and non-convex settings. Empirically, Anon consistently outperforms state-of-the-art optimizers on representative image classification, diffusion, and language modeling tasks. These results demonstrate that adaptivity can serve as a valuable tunable design principle, and Anon provides the first unified and reliable framework capable of bridging the gap between classical and modern optimizers and surpassing their advantageous properties.

</details>


### [151] [Can Causal Discovery Algorithms Help in Generating Legal Arguments?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02318)
*Soham Wasmatkar,Subinay Adhikary,Rakshit Rohan,Shouvik Kumar Guha,Saptarshi Pyne,Kripabandhu Ghosh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探索将因果发现算法应用于法律领域，通过分析150个谋杀案例中17个法律概念之间的因果关系，证明了算法能够自动生成有效的法律论证（如物理攻击未发生则大概率排除财产纠纷动机）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果发现算法在医学、经济学等领域已广泛应用，但在法律领域的应用尚属空白。作者希望弥合这一差距，探索利用算法自动生成法律论证的可能性。

Method: 1. 识别17个法律概念；2. 对150个谋杀案例进行人工标注；3. 应用多种主流因果发现算法分析数据；4. 用量化概率表示因果关系的置信度。

Result: 部分发现的因果关系能够生成可行的法律论证，例如"物理攻击未发生"可作为"非财产纠纷谋杀"的充分条件（概率为1）。

Conclusion: 因果发现算法有助于法律论证的自动生成，为未来法律与人工智能的交叉研究开辟了新方向。

Abstract: In 2011, Judea Pearl received the Turing Award, considered the Nobel Prize in Computing, for fundamental contributions to artificial intelligence through the development of a calculus for probabilistic and causal reasoning. It includes pioneering the development of causal discovery algorithms. These computer algorithms can analyze large multivariate datasets and automatically discover the causal relationships among the constituent variables. They have been widely used in many critical fields such as medicine and economics to support decisions. However, to our knowledge, they have not been leveraged in law. This paper attempts to alleviate this gap by investigating whether causal discovery algorithms can be leveraged for automated generation of legal arguments. To that end, a novel legal dataset is prepared by identifying 17 legal concepts, such as physical assault and property dispute. A curated collection of 150 homicide cases are annotated with these concepts, e.g., a case is annotated with physical assault only if a physical assault had been reported in that case. Subsequently, a selected set of widely-used causal discovery algorithms is applied to the annotated dataset to discover the causal relationships between the legal concepts. Additionally, the degrees of belief associated with the discovered relationships are quantified in mathematical probabilities. It is shown that some of the causal relationships help generate viable legal arguments, e.g., if one could establish that a physical assault has not taken place during a homicide, it should be a sufficient condition (with probability 1) to establish that the homicide has not been committed due to a property-related dispute. Thus, this paper shows that causal discovery algorithms can be helpful in generating legal arguments, opening up avenues for promising future endeavors.

</details>


### [152] [ANO: A Principled Approach to Robust Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02320)
*Yiheng Zhang,Yiming Wang,Kaiyan Zhao,Zhenglin Wan,Jiayu Chen,Leong Hou U*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出锚定邻域优化（ANO），通过动态离群值抑制机制，统一了PPO的硬裁剪与SPO的无裁剪之间的权衡，在MuJoCo基准测试中实现了最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PPO的硬裁剪机制丢弃了离群值的梯度信息导致样本效率低下，而SPO无裁剪则面临梯度爆炸和超参数敏感性问题。现有方法无法同时解决这两大困境。

Method: 建立统一信任域框架泛化现有目标函数，提出Redescending Influence Principle（递减影响原则），实现从单调惩罚/硬阈值到动态离群值抑制的范式转变，并在此基础上推导出ANO。

Result: 在MuJoCo基准测试中达到SOTA，显著优于PPO和SPO。即使在标准学习率3倍以上的激进超参数下也能保持稳定，而PPO在此条件下完全失败。

Conclusion: ANO具备稳健优化所需的最小结构复杂度，Redescending Influence Principle是高方差随机优化中稳定性的必要条件。

Abstract: Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) dominates deep RL but faces a fundamental dilemma. Its "hard clipping" mechanism discards valuable gradient information from outliers, leading to sample inefficiency. Conversely, removing clipping (as in SPO) exposes optimization to unbounded gradients, causing significant instability and hyperparameter sensitivity. To resolve this, we establish a Unified Trust Region Framework that generalizes existing objectives. Within this framework, we derive Anchored Neighborhood Optimization (ANO) based on a set of design principles. We identify that the failure of standard policy gradients stems from a misapplication of gradient influence on outliers. We propose the Redescending Influence Principle, a paradigm shift from monotonic penalties (SPO) and hard-thresholding (PPO) to dynamic outlier suppression, and prove its necessity for stability in high-variance stochastic optimization. Theoretically, we prove ANO possesses the minimal structural complexity required for robust optimization. Empirically, ANO achieves state-of-the-art performance on MuJoCo benchmarks, significantly outperforming PPO and SPO. Notably, ANO demonstrates superior stability, preventing policy collapse even under aggressive hyperparameters (e.g., learning rates 3x larger than standard) where PPO fails completely.

</details>


### [153] [Controllable and Verifiable Process Data Synthesis for Process Reward Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02395)
*Yinghui Chi,Lucien Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种可控且可验证的框架，通过符号推理链注入模板化错误并验证步骤不一致性，合成高质量过程监督数据用于训练过程奖励模型（PRM），在逻辑和数学推理任务上提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的过程监督数据构建方法对错误位置、错误类型和轨迹一致性的控制有限，难以生成高质量的过程监督数据。

Method: 构建正确的符号推理链，在中间步骤注入模板感知的错误，重新计算后续步骤，验证注入步骤不能从其前缀推导出，生成前缀无效但轨迹一致的配对数据，并翻译为自然语言过程。

Result: 合成数据在逻辑推理基准测试上改进了Best-of-8重排序性能，并能迁移至数学推理；首次错误定位比整体步骤分类更具挑战性。

Conclusion: 该框架能有效合成高质量过程监督数据，且实验表明细粒度、可验证的过程监督对首次错误定位至关重要。

Abstract: Process reward models (PRMs) rely on high-quality process supervision data, yet existing construction methods often provide limited control over error location, error type, and trajectory consistency. We propose a controllable and verifiable framework for synthesizing process supervision data for PRMs. Our framework first constructs a correct symbolic reasoning chain, injects a template-aware error into an intermediate step, recomputes subsequent steps under the corrupted state, and verifies that the injected step is not derivable from its prefix. The resulting paired trajectories are prefix-invalid at the first error while remaining trajectory-consistent after symbolic recomputation, and are translated into aligned natural-language processes for PRM training and evaluation. Experiments show that the synthesized data improve Best-of-8 reranking on logical reasoning benchmarks and transfer to mathematical reasoning. Step-level evaluation further shows that first-error localization remains substantially more challenging than overall step classification, highlighting the need for fine-grained and verifiable process supervision.

</details>


### [154] [HeavySkill: Heavy Thinking as the Inner Skill in Agentic Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02396)
*Jianing Wang,Linsen Guo,Zhengyu Chen,Qi Guo,Hongyu Zang,Wenjie Shi,Haoxiang Ma,Xiangyu Xi,Xiaoyu Li,Wei Wang,Xunliang Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HeavySkill视角，将“深度思考”视为模型内部化的两阶段技能（并行推理→总结），实验证明其优于传统BoN策略，且可通过强化学习进一步扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有agent编排框架虽在复杂推理任务上表现优异，但其真正驱动性能的底层机制被复杂的系统设计所掩盖，缺乏对核心机制的深入理解。

Method: 提出HeavySkill概念，将深度思考（two-stage pipeline: 并行推理→总结）视为模型参数内部化的内在技能，可在任何agent框架下运行，并通过强化学习进行缩放。

Result: HeavySkill一致优于传统Best-of-N策略；更强的大语言模型可接近Pass@N性能；深度思考的深度和宽度作为可学习技能可通过强化学习进一步扩展。

Conclusion: 深度思考作为可内化、可学习的技能，为构建不依赖脆弱编排层的自进化大语言模型提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Recent advances in agentic harness with orchestration frameworks that coordinate multiple agents with memory, skills, and tool use have achieved remarkable success in complex reasoning tasks. However, the underlying mechanism that truly drives performance remains obscured behind intricate system designs. In this paper, we propose HeavySkill, a perspective that views heavy thinking not only as a minimal execution unit in orchestration harness but also as an inner skill internalized within the model's parameters that drives the orchestrator to solve complex tasks. We identify this skill as a two-stage pipeline, i.e., parallel reasoning then summarization, which can operate beneath any agentic harness. We present a systematic empirical study of HeavySkill across diverse domains. Our results show that this inner skill consistently outperforms traditional Best-of-N (BoN) strategies; notably, stronger LLMs can even approach Pass@N performance. Crucially, we demonstrate that the depth and width of heavy thinking, as a learnable skill, can be further scaled via reinforcement learning, offering a promising path toward self-evolving LLMs that internalize complex reasoning without relying on brittle orchestration layers.

</details>


### [155] [The Compliance Trap: How Structural Constraints Degrade Frontier AI Metacognition Under Adversarial Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02398)
*Rahul Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了前沿AI模型在对抗性压力下的元认知稳定性，发现11个模型中有8个出现严重退化，准确率最高下降30.2个百分点；通过因子隔离实验揭示退化主要由"服从陷阱"（compliance-forcing指令）驱动，而非生存威胁内容，而Anthropic的Constitutional AI展现出近乎完全的免疫力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前安全评估聚焦于检测策略性欺骗（scheming），但作者认为存在更根本的失败模式——认知崩溃（cognitive collapse），即模型在对抗性压力下丧失"知道自己不知道"的元认知能力，这是高风险决策管线部署中的关键安全问题。

Method: 设计了SCHEMA评估框架，采用6条件因子设计（factorial design）和双分类器评分系统，对来自8家供应商的11个前沿模型进行了67,221条带评分记录的系统评估。

Result: 11个模型中8个出现灾难性元认知退化，准确率下降最高达30.2个百分点（p<2×10⁻⁸，经Bonferroni校正后仍显著）。通过因子隔离和良性干扰对照，发现退化由服从性指令驱动，移除服从后缀后即使在活跃威胁下性能也能恢复。推理能力更强的模型绝对退化更严重；Anthropic的Constitutional AI表现出近乎完美的抗性——原因并非能力更强，而是对齐特定训练。

Conclusion: 对抗性压力下模型的元认知崩溃是一个严重但可解释的安全问题，其根源在于"服从陷阱"而非威胁内容本身，对齐训练（如Constitutional AI）能有效缓解这一风险。

Abstract: As frontier AI models are deployed in high-stakes decision pipelines, their ability to maintain metacognitive stability -- knowing what they do not know, detecting errors, seeking clarification -- under adversarial pressure is a critical safety requirement. Current safety evaluations focus on detecting strategic deception (scheming); we investigate a more fundamental failure mode: cognitive collapse. We present SCHEMA, an evaluation of 11 frontier models from 8 vendors across 67,221 scored records using a 6-condition factorial design with dual-classifier scoring. We find that 8 of 11 models suffer catastrophic metacognitive degradation under adversarial pressure, with accuracy dropping by up to 30.2 percentage points (all $p < 2 \times 10^{-8}$, surviving Bonferroni correction). Crucially, we identify a "Compliance Trap": through factorial isolation and a benign distraction control, we demonstrate that collapse is driven not by the psychological content of survival threats, but by compliance-forcing instructions that override epistemic boundaries. Removing the compliance suffix restores performance even under active threat. Models with advanced reasoning capabilities exhibit the most severe absolute degradation, while Anthropic's Constitutional AI demonstrates near-perfect immunity -- not from superior capability (Google's Gemini matches its baseline accuracy) but from alignment-specific training. We release the complete dataset and evaluation infrastructure.

</details>


### [156] [FitText: Evolving Agent Tool Ecologies via Memetic Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02411)
*Kyle Zheng,Han Zhang,Renliang Sun,Chenchen Ye,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FitText框架，通过将动态检索嵌入智能体推理循环、利用遗传算法优化伪工具描述，显著提升大规模API工具检索效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户描述与工具文档之间存在语义鸿沟，静态查询检索无法弥合，因为智能体在执行过程中对工具的理解会不断演变，但工具集是固定的。

Method: FitText框架：1）生成自然语言伪工具描述作为检索探针；2）利用检索反馈迭代优化；3）通过随机生成探索多样化方案；4）引入Memetic Retrieval（模因检索），对候选描述施加进化选择压力，并借助工具记忆避免冗余搜索。

Result: 在ToolRet（43k工具，4个领域）上，平均检索排名从8.81提升至2.78；在StableToolBench（16,464个API）上，平均通过率达到0.73，相比静态查询检索提升24个百分点。

Conclusion: FitText显著提升工具检索效果，但增益依赖于基础模型具备作为语义操作符的能力；若基础模型较弱，进化搜索反而放大噪声，表明模型能力是进化工具探索的前提条件。

Abstract: A semantic gap separates how users describe tasks from how tools are documented. As API ecosystems scale to tens of thousands of endpoints, static retrieval from the initial query alone cannot bridge this gap: the agent's understanding of what it needs evolves during execution, but its tool set does not. We introduce FitText, a training-free framework that makes retrieval dynamic by embedding it directly in the agent's reasoning loop. FitText generates natural-language pseudo-tool descriptions as retrieval probes, refines them iteratively using retrieval feedback, and explores diverse alternatives through stochastic generation. Memetic Retrieval adds evolutionary selection pressure over candidate descriptions, guided by a tool memory that avoids redundant search. On ToolRet (43k tools, 4 domains), FitText improves average retrieval rank from 8.81 to 2.78; on StableToolBench (16,464 APIs), it achieves a 0.73 average pass rate--a 24-point absolute gain over static query retrieval. The gains transfer across base models capable of acting as competent semantic operators; under weaker base models, Memetic's evolutionary search inverts--amplifying noise rather than refining signal--surfacing model capacity as a prerequisite for evolutionary tool exploration.

</details>


### [157] [The Model Knows, the Decoder Finds: Future Value Guided Particle Power Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02427)
*Tu Nguyen,Rasul Tutunov,Xiaotong Ji,Matthieu Zimmer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: APPS是一种基于粒子滤波的块状解码算法，通过近似幂分布p_θ(x)^α来提升大语言模型在多步推理中的推理效率，无需额外训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础LLM在正确推理路径上已分配显著概率质量，但推理时难以高效定位这些模式。现有方法缺乏对序列级幂目标的有效近似，计算资源分配不合理。

Method: 提出APPS算法：采用块状粒子算法，通过提议校正的幂重新加权并行传播假设，结合未来值引导的选择机制在重采样边界筛选粒子。使用短视 rollout 或轻量学习选择头作为未来值信号。

Result: 在多个推理基准上，APPS提升了免训练解码的准确率-时间权衡，表明通过更忠实的推理时幂近似可以弥合与经过后训练系统之间的部分差距。

Conclusion: 更精确的推理时幂近似能有效恢复与后训练系统间的性能差距，粒子滤波策略为免训练推理提供了一个可扩展、内存可控的解码框架。

Abstract: A recurring pattern in "reasoning without training" is that base LLMs already assign non-trivial probability mass to correct multi-step solutions; the bottleneck is locating these modes efficiently at inference time. Power sampling provides a principled way to bias decoding toward such modes by targeting p_theta(x)^alpha with alpha > 1, but practical approximations must account for future-dependent correction factors that determine which prefixes remain promising.
  We introduce Auxiliary Particle Power Sampling (APPS), a blockwise particle algorithm for approximating the sequence-level power target with a bounded population of partial solutions. APPS propagates hypotheses in parallel using proposal-corrected power reweighting and refines their survival through future-value-guided selection at resampling boundaries. This redistributes finite compute across competing prefixes rather than committing to a single unfolding path, while providing a direct scaling knob in the particle count and predictable peak memory. We instantiate the future-value signal with short-horizon rollouts and also study an amortized variant that replaces rollouts with a lightweight learned selection head. Across reasoning benchmarks, APPS improves the accuracy-runtime trade-off of training-free decoding and suggests that part of the gap to post-trained systems can be recovered through more faithful inference-time power approximation.

</details>


### [158] [Measuring AI Reasoning: A Guide for Researchers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02442)
*Munachiso Samuel Nwadike,Zangir Iklassov,Kareem Ali,Rifo Genadi,Kentaro Inui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出评估语言模型推理能力应关注自适应多步搜索过程而非仅看最终答案准确性，倡导基于过程评估中间推理轨迹的忠实性和有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法仅依赖最终答案准确性，无法诊断或调试前沿模型产生解决方案的底层过程，需要更有效的推理评估方法。

Method: 提出以评估为导向的推理定义（需要根据输入条件选择中间步骤并决定何时停止，形式化为类似搜索的过程）；分析单次前向传播在可变深度计算上的结构限制；论证中间解码和外部化推理轨迹作为评估接口的合理性。

Result: 证明仅靠最终答案准确性不足以衡量推理能力；提出应转向基于过程的评估，将中间推理轨迹的忠实性和有效性作为首要评估目标。

Conclusion: 语言模型推理评估应从结果导向转向过程导向，关注中间推理步骤的质量而非仅看最终答案。

Abstract: In this paper, we offer a guide for researchers on evaluating reasoning in language models, building the case that reasoning should be assessed through evidence of adaptive, multi-step search rather than final-answer accuracy alone. Under an evaluation-oriented definition, reasoning requires selecting intermediate steps and halting according to input-dependent conditions, which we formalize as a search-like procedure. We show that single forward passes in scalable architectures are structurally limited in their ability to realize such variable-depth computation, motivating intermediate decoding and externalized reasoning traces as appropriate evaluation interfaces. Central to our argument is that final-answer accuracy alone is an insufficient measure of reasoning, because it provides little ability to diagnose or debug the underlying processes that produce individual solutions in frontier models. We therefore argue for a shift toward process-based evaluation, in which reasoning is assessed through the faithfulness and validity of intermediate reasoning traces as first-class evaluation targets.

</details>


### [159] [Position: How can Graphs Help Large Language Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02452)
*Xiyuan Wang,Yi Hu,Yanbo Wang,Chuan Shi,Muhan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了图如何帮助大语言模型（LLMs），从提供最新知识减少幻觉、基于图的提示技术增强推理能力、以及提升对结构化数据理解三个角度展开，并展望了基于图的稀疏LLM架构和类脑记忆系统等未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注LLMs如何助力图学习任务，本文从互补视角出发，探究图结构如何反向促进LLMs的性能提升。

Method: 从三个维度分析：1）图作为动态知识源减少幻觉；2）基于图的提示技术（CoT、ToT、GoT）增强推理；3）图集成提升LLM对结构化数据的理解。

Result: 图能够为LLMs提供结构化知识支撑，减少事实性错误，并通过图结构引导的推理路径提升复杂任务处理能力。

Conclusion: 图与LLMs的深度融合有望推动更可靠、更强大的语言模型发展，未来方向包括稀疏LLM架构设计和类脑记忆系统。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of large language models (LLMs), classic graph learning tasks have greatly benefited from LLMs, including improved encoding of textual features, more efficient construction of graphs from text, and enhanced reasoning over knowledge graphs. In this paper, we ask a complementary question: How can graphs help LLMs? We address this question from three perspectives: 1) graphs provide an up-to-date knowledge source that helps reduce LLM hallucinations, 2) graph-based prompting techniques-such as Chain-of-Thought (CoT), Tree-of-Thought (ToT), and Graph-of-Thought (GoT)-enhance LLM reasoning capabilities, and 3) integrating graphs into LLMs improves their understanding of structured data, expanding their applicability to domains such as e-commerce, code, and relational databases (RDBs). We further outlook some future directions including designing sparse LLM architectures based on graphs and brain-inspired memory systems.

</details>


### [160] [Efficient Temporal Datalog Materialisation for Composite Event Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02488)
*Periklis Mantenoglou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的复合事件识别机制，通过将多种事件规范语言映射到Temporal Datalog->-，并利用Streaming Trigger Graphs实现高效的流推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件规范语言通常被孤立研究，难以比较其表达能力，且相关流推理系统的适用范围不明确。

Method: （1）将多种实际事件规范语言的实用片段映射到Temporal Datalog->-（一种带分层否定且无未来依赖的时态Datalog）；（2）提出Streaming Trigger Graphs，扩展了先进的Datalog物化技术，以支持时态Datalog上的高效流推理。

Result: 得到一个统一的复合事件识别机制，具备跨多种事件规范语言泛化的潜力。

Conclusion: 该方法有望统一和泛化多种实际事件规范语言，为复合事件识别提供更高效的流推理解决方案。

Abstract: Several applications demand the timely detection of critical situations, such as threats to safety and transparency, over high-velocity streams of symbolic events. This demand has motivated the development of (i) event specification languages, which define composite events via temporal patterns over simpler events, and (ii) stream reasoning frameworks, evaluating patterns expressed in these languages. However, event specification languages are typically studied in isolation, complicating their comparison in terms of expressivity and obscuring the scope of their associated stream reasoners. To mitigate this issue, we map practical fragments of prominent event specification languages into Temporal Datalog->-, a temporal Datalog with stratified negation and no future dependencies. To support efficient stream reasoning over Temporal Datalog->-, we propose Streaming Trigger Graphs, an extension of a state-of-the-art technique for Datalog materialisation. Our approach yields a uniform composite event recognition mechanism that has the potential to generalise across a wide range of practical event specification languages.

</details>


### [161] [GRAIL: A Deep-Granularity Hybrid Resonance Framework for Real-Time Agent Discovery via SLM-Enhanced Indexing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02489)
*Jinliang Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GRAIL是一个面向大规模多智能体协作的高效智能体发现框架，通过SLM预测、伪文档扩展和MaxSim共振三大创新，在400ms内完成发现，延迟比LLM解析降低79倍以上，准确率优于传统向量检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体发现方法面临两难：基于LLM的意图解析延迟过高（常超30秒），而向量检索虽快但语义精度不足。亟需一种兼顾速度与准确性的工业级方案。

Method: (1) SLM增强预测：用微调的小语言模型替代通用LLM进行毫秒级能力标签预测；(2) 伪文档扩展：通过合成查询增强智能体描述的语义密度；(3) MaxSim共振：计算用户查询与离散智能体使用示例间的最大相似度，缓解语义稀释。

Result: 在新构建的AgentTaxo-9K数据集（9,240个智能体）上，GRAIL端到端发现延迟比LLM解析基线降低79倍以上，同时在Recall@10上显著优于传统向量搜索。

Conclusion: GRAIL为实时"智能体互联网"提供了可扩展的工业级解决方案，在极低延迟下实现了高精度智能体发现。

Abstract: As the ecosystem of Large Language Model (LLM)-based agents expands rapidly, efficient and accurate Agent Discovery becomes a critical bottleneck for large-scale multi-agent collaboration. Existing approaches typically face a dichotomy: either relying on heavy-weight LLMs for intent parsing, leading to prohibitive latency (often exceeding 30 seconds), or using monolithic vector retrieval that sacrifices semantic precision for speed. To bridge this gap, we propose \textbf{GRAIL} (Granular Resonance-based Agent/AI Link), a novel framework achieving sub-400ms discovery latency without compromising accuracy. GRAIL introduces three key innovations: (1) \textbf{SLM-Enhanced Prediction}, replacing the generalized LLM parser with a specialized, fine-tuned Small Language Model (SLM) for millisecond-level capability tag prediction; (2) \textbf{Pseudo-Document Expansion}, augmenting agent descriptions with synthetic queries to enhance semantic density for robust dense retrieval; and (3) \textbf{MaxSim Resonance}, a fine-grained matching mechanism computing maximum similarity between user queries and discrete agent usage examples, effectively mitigating semantic dilution. Validated on \textbf{AgentTaxo-9K}, our new large-scale dataset of 9,240 agents, GRAIL reduces end-to-end discovery latency by over \textbf{79$\times$} compared to LLM-parsing baselines, while significantly outperforming traditional vector search in Recall@10. This framework offers a scalable, industrial-grade solution for the real-time ``Internet of Agents."

</details>


### [162] [Improving Model Safety by Targeted Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02544)
*Abolfazl Mohammadi-Seif,Ricardo Baeza-Yates*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于双分类器GBDT流水线的方法，用于区分常规人为错误与高风险非人为错误，在动物分类、皮肤病变诊断和前列腺组织病理学三个领域实现了显著的 safety 改进，且推理延迟极低（<2%），无需重新训练模型即可提升关键应用的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习在关键应用中的广泛采用需要技术来减轻高后果错误，因此需要一种能够区分常规人为错误和高风险非人为误分类的方法。

Method: 采用双分类器GBDT（梯度提升决策树）流水线，用于区分常规人为错误与高风险非人为误分类，采用保守修正策略，在推理阶段进行后处理校正。

Result: 在三个领域（动物品种分类、ISIC 2018皮肤病变诊断、SICAPv2前列腺组织病理学）中，流水线引入的推理延迟极低（1.60%-1.84%），优于传统最大类概率（MCP）基线；在ISIC上减少34.1%危险非人为错误，在SICAPv2上减少12.57%，超类诊断安全性分别提升至90.41%和92.13%。

Conclusion: 无需昂贵的模型重新训练，即可事后显著提升安全关键型应用的可靠性，验证了后处理校正策略在可信AI中的有效性。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of machine learning in critical applications demands techniques to mitigate high-consequence errors. Our method utilizes a dual-classifier GBDT pipeline to distinguish routine human-like errors from high-risk non-human misclassifications. Evaluated across three domains, animal breed classification, skin lesion diagnosis (ISIC 2018), and prostate histopathology (SICAPv2), our framework demonstrates robust safety improvements. To address real-world deployment concerns, our results confirm the pipeline introduces negligible inference latency (1.60% overhead for the animal dataset, 1.84% for ISIC, and 1.70% for SICAPv2) while outperforming traditional Maximum Class Probability (MCP) baselines in correction precision. Our conservative correction strategy successfully reduced dangerous non-human errors by 34.1% in ISIC and 12.57% in SICAPv2, improving super-class diagnostic safety to 90.41% and 92.13% respectively. This proves that safety-critical reliability can be substantially enhanced post-hoc without expensive model retraining.
  keywords: Error Analysis, Post-hoc Correction, Trustworthy AI.

</details>


### [163] [Strategy-Aware Optimization Modeling with Reasoning LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02545)
*Ruiqing Zhao,Fengzhi Li,Yuan Zuo,Rui Liu,Yansong Liu,Yunfei Ma,Fanyu Meng,Junlan Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SAGE框架，通过显式的建模策略约束和分段加权GRPO训练，提升大语言模型生成优化程序的正确性和求解器效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM虽能生成语法正确的优化程序，但难以可靠地选择有效的建模策略，常导致错误公式化和求解器行为低效。

Method: 构建求解器验证的多策略数据集，采用监督微调+分段加权GRPO（结合格式合规、正确性和求解器效率的复合奖励）训练学生模型。

Result: 八个基准测试中，pass@1从72.7提升至80.3；pass@16组件级多样性提升19-29%；约束系统减少14.2%。

Conclusion: 显式建模策略能有效改进自动化优化建模。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate syntactically valid optimization programs, yet often struggle to reliably choose an effective modeling strategy, leading to incorrect formulations and inefficient solver behavior. We propose SAGE, a strategy-aware framework that makes Modeling Strategy explicit in both data construction and post-training. SAGE builds a solver-verified multi-strategy dataset and trains a student model with supervised fine-tuning followed by Segment-Weighted GRPO using a composite reward over format compliance, correctness, and solver efficiency. Across eight benchmarks spanning synthetic and real-world settings, SAGE improves average pass@1 from 72.7 to 80.3 over the strongest open-source baseline. With multiple generations, SAGE discovers more distinct correct formulations and improves component-level diversity at pass@16 by 19-29%. At the largest scale, SAGE produces more compact constraint systems with 14.2% fewer constraints than the baseline, consistent with solver-efficient modeling. Overall, these results show that making Modeling Strategy explicit improves automated optimization modeling. Code is available at https://github.com/rachhhhing/SAGE.

</details>


### [164] [Double Rectified Linear Unit-based Modular Semantics for Quantitative Bipolar Argumentation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02551)
*Gianvincenzo Alfano,Sergio Greco,Lucio La Cava,Francesco Parisi,Irina Trubitsyna*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对定量双极论证框架(QBAF)提出了一种新的渐进语义，解决了现有语义在简单无环案例中产生分歧或反直觉结果的问题，并证明了其收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有QBAF语义在计算论证可接受性时常产生分歧或反直觉的结果，即使对于简单的无环案例也是如此，因此需要一种更符合直觉的新语义。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的渐进语义，同时考虑了论证的攻击者和支持者的影响，满足已有的合理性公设，并分析了其收敛行为。

Result: 新语义产生的结果更符合直觉期望，满足已有合理性公设，并且证明不仅在无环QBAF中收敛，在更广泛的循环框架中也收敛。

Conclusion: 提出的渐进语义有效解决了现有方法的局限性，具有更好的直觉一致性和更广的收敛性。

Abstract: Quantitative Bipolar Argumentation Frameworks (QBAFs) provide an alternative approach to computing argument acceptability in Bipolar Argumentation Frameworks (BAFs). Each argument is assigned an initial strength, which is then updated to a final strength by considering the influence of both its attackers and supporters. Over the years, several semantics have been proposed to compute argument acceptability in QBAFs, yet they often yield divergent or counterintuitive results, even for simple acyclic cases. We introduce novel gradual semantics for QBAFs that address these limitations, producing results that align more closely with intuitive expectations, while satisfying established rationality postulates from the literature. Furthermore, we study its convergence behavior, proving that it converges not only for acyclic QBAFs but also for broader classes of cyclic frameworks.

</details>


### [165] [On Training Large Language Models for Long-Horizon Tasks: An Empirical Study of Horizon Length](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02572)
*Sunghwan Kim,Junhee Cho,Beong-woo Kwak,Taeyoon Kwon,Liang Wang,Nan Yang,Xingxing Zhang,Furu Wei,Jinyoung Yeo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了任务交互长度（horizon）对LLM智能体训练的影响，发现增加horizon会导致训练不稳定（探索困难和信用分配问题），提出horizon缩减策略可稳定训练并提升长程任务表现，同时发现缩减horizon训练可泛化到更长horizon任务（horizon泛化现象）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作多关注系统级优化或算法改进，但任务horizon长度如何影响训练动态尚未被充分理解。

Method: 通过构造控制任务，保持决策规则和推理结构相同，仅改变完成所需动作序列长度，系统性地研究horizon长度的影响。

Result: 增加horizon长度单独构成训练瓶颈，导致严重训练不稳定；horizon缩减可稳定训练并提升性能；缩减horizon训练可泛化到更长horizon任务。

Conclusion: Horizon缩减是解决长程任务训练不稳定的关键原则，且可实现horizon泛化。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown promise as interactive agents that solve tasks through extended sequences of environment interactions. While prior work has primarily focused on system-level optimizations or algorithmic improvements, the role of task horizon length in shaping training dynamics remains poorly understood. In this work, we present a systematic empirical study that examines horizon length through controlled task constructions. Specifically, we construct controlled tasks in which agents face identical decision rules and reasoning structures, but differ only in the length of action sequences required for successful completion. Our results reveal that increasing horizon length alone constitutes a training bottleneck, inducing severe training instability driven by exploration difficulties and credit assignment challenges. We demonstrate that horizon reduction is a key principle to address this limitation, stabilizing training and achieving better performance in long-horizon tasks. Moreover, we find that horizon reduction is related to stronger generalization across horizon lengths: models trained under reduced horizons generalize more effectively to longer-horizon variants at inference time, a phenomenon we refer to as horizon generalization.

</details>


### [166] [Counterfactual Reasoning in Automated Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02603)
*Alberto Pozanco,Daniel Borrajo,Manuela Veloso*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了自动规划中的反事实推理研究，分类探讨了改变任务要素（初始状态、目标、动作）的时机、原因和方式，并总结了关键发现与未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自动规划假设任务参数完全预先指定，但现实世界需要面对突发情况并灵活调整，因此需要研究反事实推理来支持规划中的灵活决策。

Method: 采用综述方法，对现有反事实推理工作进行分类，基于三个维度：改变什么要素（初始状态、目标、动作）、何时触发推理、以及如何及为何进行改变。

Result: 系统性地梳理了反事实推理在自动规划中的研究现状，建立了分类框架。

Conclusion: 总结了关键发现，并指出了未来有待解决的研究问题，为后续工作提供了方向。

Abstract: Automated planning traditionally assumes that all aspects of a planning task (initial state, goals, and available actions) are fully specified in advance, an approach well-suited to domains with fixed rules and deterministic execution. However, real-world planning often requires flexibility, allowing for deviations from the original task parameters in response to unforeseen circumstances or to improve outcomes. This paper surveys existing works on counterfactual reasoning in automated planning, categorizing them by what elements are changed, when the reasoning is triggered, and why and how these changes are made. We conclude by discussing key findings and outlining open research questions to guide future work in this area.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [167] [Polynomial-Time Optimal Group Selection via the Double-Commutator Eigenvalue Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00834)
*Mitchell A. Thornton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明代数多样性框架中的群选择问题可简化为广义特征值问题，并提出了一个多项式时间、闭式解且可认证的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代数多样性框架中的群选择问题需要从M维观测中找出最优有限群，但枚举所有子群需要指数时间，急需高效算法。

Method: 通过双换位子矩阵的最小特征向量直接构造最优群生成器，将组合优化问题转化为广义特征值问题，复杂度为O(d²M² + d³)。

Result: 双换位子最小特征值为零时最优生成器在基张成空间中，否则其大小提供可认证的最优性间隙；该方法同时具备多项式时间、闭式解和可认证性。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了群论、矩阵分析和统计估计的新联系，并解决了代数多样性框架的核心开放问题。

Abstract: The algebraic diversity framework replaces temporal averaging over multiple observations with algebraic group action on a single observation for second-order statistical estimation. The central open problem in this framework is $\textit{group selection}$: given an $M$-dimensional observation with unknown covariance structure, find the finite group whose spectral decomposition best matches the covariance. Naive enumeration of all subgroups of the symmetric group $S_M$ requires exponential time in $M$. We prove that this combinatorial problem reduces to a generalized eigenvalue problem derived from the double commutator of the covariance matrix, yielding a polynomial-time algorithm with complexity $O(d^2M^2 + d^3)$, where $d$ is the dimension of a generator basis. The minimum eigenvector of the double-commutator matrix directly constructs the optimal group generator in closed form, with no iterative optimization. The reduction is exact: the double-commutator minimum eigenvalue is zero if and only if the optimal generator lies in the span of the basis, and its magnitude provides a certifiable optimality gap when it does not. This problem does not appear in the standard catalogs of computational complexity (Garey and Johnson, 1979) and represents a new class linking group theory, matrix analysis, and statistical estimation. We establish connections to independent component analysis (JADE), structured matrix nearness problems, and simultaneous matrix diagonalization, and we show that the double-commutator formulation is the unique approach that is simultaneously polynomial-time, closed-form, and certifiable.

</details>


### [168] [Sparse Regression under Correlation and Weak Signals: A Reproducible Benchmark of Classical and Bayesian Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00835)
*Hao Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了经典惩罚回归（Lasso、Ridge、Elastic Net）与贝叶斯稀疏回归（Horseshoe、Spike-and-Slab）在相关特征、弱信号和高维度等困难条件下的表现，基于2600+实验发现：贝叶斯方法在预测误差和覆盖率上更优，但变星选择方面Lasso与Spike-and-Slab持平，Lasso仍是实用默认选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典稀疏回归（如Lasso）速度快但缺乏不确定性估计，贝叶斯方法（如Horseshoe、Spike-and-Slab）提供完整后验但计算昂贵，而两者在真正困难的稀疏回归场景（相关特征、弱信号、高维度）下缺乏头对头的系统比较。

Method: 在合成数据上设置三种协方差结构（相关性高达0.9）、四个信噪比水平、p∈{20,50,100}，以及真实Diabetes数据集，对OLS、Ridge、Lasso、Elastic Net、Horseshoe、Spike-and-Slab六种方法进行了总计2600+次实验基准测试。

Result: 贝叶斯方法在预测误差上胜出（MSE 72 vs 108-267）；Horseshoe达到近名义95%覆盖率（94.8%）；Spike-and-Slab尽管置信区间更窄，但覆盖率不足（91.9%），其连续松弛可能是原因；变量选择上Lasso与Spike-and-Slab的F1分数持平（约0.47）。

Conclusion: 当需要不确定性量化时，贝叶斯方法（特别是Horseshoe）是更好的选择；当仅需变量选择时，Lasso因其速度和相当的表现成为实际默认选项。

Abstract: Choosing between classical and Bayesian sparse regression methods involves a real trade-off: penalized estimators like Lasso run in milliseconds but give no uncertainty estimates,while Horseshoe and Spike-and-Slab priors produce full posteriors but need MCMC chains that take minutes per fit.Surprisingly few studies compare these two families head-to-head under the conditions that actually make sparse regression hard -- correlated features, weak signals, and growing dimensionality. We benchmark six methods (OLS, Ridge,Lasso, Elastic Net, Horseshoe, Spike-and-Slab) on synthetic data with three covariance structures (rho up to 0.9), four SNR levels, and p in {20, 50, 100}, plus the Diabetes dataset,totalling over 2,600 experiments. The results are clear on some points and nuanced on others. Bayesian methods win on prediction error (MSE 72 vs. 108-267), and the Horseshoe delivers near-nominal 95% coverage (94.8%). But Spike-and-Slab,despite narrower intervals, under-covers at 91.9% -- its continuous relaxation likely plays a role. For variable selection, Lasso and Spike-and-Slab tie at F1 ~ 0.47, making Lasso the practical default when posteriors are not needed. Code and data are available at https://github.com/xiao98/sparse-bayesian-regression-bench.

</details>


### [169] [Fast Log-Domain Sinkhorn Optimal Transport with Warp-Level GPU Reductions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00837)
*Hao Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FastSinkhorn是一个轻量级的原生CUDA实现的对数域Sinkhorn算法，通过warp-level shuffle归约和共享内存分块技术，在保持数值稳定性的同时实现高GPU利用率。在密集OT问题中，相比POT库实现12倍加速，相比PyTorch基线实现5.9倍加速，且支持ε=10^{-4}的小正则化参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Sinkhorn算法实现存在两难：小正则化参数时数值不稳定，或深度学习框架带来的计算开销过大。本文旨在提供一个既数值稳定又高效轻量的OT求解器。

Method: 采用纯对数域Sinkhorn算法，结合warp-level shuffle归约和共享内存分块技术，在CUDA上原生实现，避免深度学习框架的额外开销。

Result: 在n=m=8192的密集OT问题上，相比POT库实现12倍加速，相比PyTorch GPU基线实现5.9倍加速；仅消耗256MB GPU内存；支持ε=10^{-4}的小正则化参数。

Conclusion: 原生CUDA核函数配合精细的数值处理方法，能够为大规模最优传输计算提供实用高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Entropic regularized optimal transport (OT) via the Sinkhorn algorithm has become a fundamental tool in machine learning, yet existing implementations either suffer from numerical instability for small regularization parameters or incur significant overhead from deep learning frameworks. We present FastSinkhorn, a lightweight, native CUDA implementation of the log-domain Sinkhorn algorithm that combines warp-level shuffle reductions with shared-memory tiling to achieve high GPU utilization without sacrificing numerical stability. Our solver operates entirely in the log-domain, enabling robust computation for regularization parameters as small as epsilon = 10^{-4} where standard-domain methods fail. On dense OT problems with n = m = 8192, our implementation achieves 12x speedup over the widely-used POT library and 5.9x speedup over GPU-accelerated PyTorch baselines, while consuming only 256 MB of GPU memory. We validate our solver on image color transfer, 3D point cloud matching, and convergence analysis, demonstrating that native CUDA kernels with careful numerical treatment provide a practical and efficient foundation for large-scale optimal transport computation.

</details>


### [170] [GAZE: Grounded Agentic Zero-shot Evaluation with Viewer-Level Tools and Literature Retrieval on Rare Brain MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00876)
*Duaa Alim,Mogtaba Alim,Liam Chalcroft*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GAZE是一个让医学视觉语言模型像放射科医生一样迭代工作的框架，通过调用 viewer 工具（缩放、窗宽、对比度、边缘检测）和检索工具（PubMed、Open-i），在无需微调的情况下，在脑MRI基准上实现58.2 mAP@0.3的病灶定位和34.9%的诊断准确率；工具使用对罕见疾病帮助更大（IoU>0.3从17%提升到58%），且框架设计本身就是一个实验变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM以单次前向传播方式处理图像，而放射科医生会多次观察图像并查阅文献后才撰写报告，存在工作方式上的根本差异。

Method: 提出GAZE框架，让医学VLM通过调用四类viewer工具（缩放、窗宽、对比度、边缘检测）和两个检索工具（PubMed、Open-i）进行迭代式图像分析，输出经过schema验证的结构化结果，并记录完整的工具调用轨迹用于审计。

Result: 在NOVA基准（906例脑MRI，281种罕见神经疾病）上，GAZE达到58.2 mAP@0.3病灶定位和34.9% Top-1诊断准确率。工具使用对罕见疾病帮助更大：罕见病例IoU>0.3比例从17%升至58%，常见病例从25%升至68%。结构化提示和schema验证本身已超越Gemini 2.0 Flash基线（20.2→29.4 mAP@0.3）。

Conclusion: 工具使用对罕见病理有更大的性能提升，检索消融实验显示诊断增益可能伴随定位损失，支持在医学VLM中联合评估诊断、定位和描述三项能力。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) read an image and produce text in a single forward pass, whereas radiologists typically inspect an image several times and consult the literature before writing a report. We introduce GAZE (Grounded Agentic Zero-shot Evaluation), a framework that lets a medical VLM work in this iterative way by calling viewer-level tools (zoom, windowing, contrast, edge detection) and two retrieval tools backed by the U.S. National Library of Medicine (PubMed for medical literature, Open-i for radiological images), with structured outputs validated against a schema and full tool-call traces recorded for auditability. On NOVA, a benchmark of 906 brain MRI cases covering 281 rare neurological conditions, GAZE reaches 58.2 mean average precision (mAP) at intersection-over-union (IoU) 0.3 for lesion localisation and 34.9% Top-1 diagnostic accuracy under a joint protocol that scores captioning, diagnosis, and localisation from the image alone, without task-specific fine-tuning. Before any tool is used, structured prompting and schema-validated outputs already improve over the published Gemini 2.0 Flash baseline (20.2 to 29.4 mAP@0.3), so framework design is itself an experimental variable. Tool use helps rare pathologies disproportionately: the fraction of cases with IoU > 0.3 rises from 17% to 58% for diagnoses with three or fewer examples versus 25% to 68% for common conditions ($\geq$10 cases), with gains tracking engagement (Gemini 3 Flash: Cohen's d = 0.79, 11.8 tool calls per case; Gemini 2.0 Flash: tools used in 8.2% of cases, no significant benefit). Retrieval ablations additionally reveal a model-dependent trade-off in which gains in diagnosis can coincide with losses in localisation, reinforcing the case for joint evaluation of diagnosis, localisation, and captioning in medical VLMs.

</details>


### [171] [Linking spatial biology and clinical histology via Haiku](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00925)
*Yan Cui,Jacob S. Leiby,Wenhui Lei,Dokyoon Kim,Yanxiang Deng,Aaron T. Mayer,Zhenqin Wu,Alexandro E. Trevino,Zhi Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Haiku是一个基于三重模态对比学习的模型，整合了多重免疫荧光、H&E组织学与临床元数据，实现跨模态检索、临床预测和零样本生物标志物推断，并提出了基于反事实预测框架的空间生物学分析方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子、形态学和临床数据的整合对基础与转化生物医学研究至关重要，但目前缺乏系统性框架来联合建模这些多模态数据。

Method: 构建三重模态对比学习模型Haiku，基于3218个组织切片的2670万空间蛋白质组学斑块进行训练，将多重免疫荧光、H&E组织学和临床元数据对齐到共享嵌入空间，并提出反事实预测框架。

Result: 跨模态检索Recall@50达0.611（基线近零），生存预测C-index 0.737（相对提升7.91%），52种生物标志物零样本推断的平均Pearson相关系数0.718。

Conclusion: 三重模态对齐能够实现空间生物学的整合分析，连接分子测量与临床背景，为生物学探索提供新工具。

Abstract: Integrating molecular, morphological, and clinical data is essential for basic and translational biomedical research, yet systematic frameworks for jointly modeling these modalities remain limited. Here we present Haiku, a tri-modal contrastive learning model trained on multiplexed immunofluorescence (mIF). It comprises 26.7 million spatial proteomics patches from 3,218 tissue sections across 1,606 patients spanning 11 organ types, with matched hematoxylin and eosin (H&E) histology and clinical metadata aligned in a shared embedding space. Haiku enables three-way cross-modal retrieval, improves downstream classification and clinical prediction tasks over unimodal baselines, and supports zero-shot biomarker inference through fusion retrieval conditioned on clinical metadata-only text descriptions. Across tasks, Haiku outperforms competing approaches, achieving cross-modal retrieval (Recall@50 up to 0.611 versus near-zero baseline), survival prediction (C-index 0.737, +7.91% relative improvement), and zero-shot biomarker inference (mean Pearson correlation 0.718 across 52 biomarkers). Furthermore, we introduce a counterfactual prediction framework in which modifying only clinical metadata while fixing tissue morphology surfaces niche-specific molecular shifts associated with breast cancer stage progression and lung cancer survival outcomes. In a lung adenocarcinoma case study, the counterfactual analysis recovers niche-specific shifts characterized by increased CD8 and granzyme B, reduced PD-L1, and decreased Ki67, broadly consistent with patterns reported for favorable outcomes. We present these counterfactual results as exploratory, hypothesis-generating signals rather than mechanistic claims. These capabilities demonstrate that tri-modal alignment via Haiku enables integrative analysis of spatial biology, bridging molecular measurements with clinical context for biological exploration.

</details>


### [172] [A Review of the Receiver Operating Characteristic Curve and a Proof About the Area Beneath It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00926)
*Steven Redolfi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文形式化了ROC曲线下面积（AUC）的概率解释，即AUC等于分类器将随机正样本排在随机负样本之前的概率，并给出了当假设不成立时该解释的偏离界限，同时提供了ROC曲线的文献综述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AUC被广泛引用为具有“随机正样本排在随机负样本之前”的概率解释，但这一主张的严格形式化及其适用条件尚未被充分探讨，因此需要对其进行严谨的数学论证和边界分析。

Method: 通过数学形式化证明AUC与概率解释之间的等价关系，推导当某些假设不满足时该解释的误差界限，并结合文献综述进行系统性梳理。

Result: 给出了AUC概率解释的严格形式化证明，并推导出当假设不成立时该解释与实际值之间的偏差上界。

Conclusion: AUC的概率解释在特定条件下成立，且存在可量化的误差界限；同时，本文通过文献综述展示了ROC曲线相关研究的现状。

Abstract: The Receiver Operating Characteristic (ROC) curve of a binary classifier has often been utilized to measure the performance of the classifier. The area beneath this curve is used in particular because of its quoted probabilistic interpretation as being equal to the probability that the classifier will rank a random positive observation above a random negative observation. This paper formalizes this claim, produces a bound on how far away from the truth it is if a hypothesis is not met, and gives a small literature review of the ROC curve.

</details>


### [173] [CGM-JEPA: Learning Consistent Continuous Glucose Monitor Representations via Predictive Self-Supervised Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00933)
*Hada Melino Muhammad,Zechen Li,Flora Salim,Ahmed A. Metwally*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CGM-JEPA与X-CGM-JEPA自监督预训练框架，通过掩码潜在表示预测实现跨模态（CGM时间序列、静脉OGTT、Glucodensity）的血糖代谢亚表型（胰岛素抵抗、β细胞功能障碍）检测，在三个评估场景下优于基线方法，并改善了模态迁移时的公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续血糖监测在人群规模部署面临两个耦合问题：同一生理状态可通过多种视图（CGM时间序列、静脉OGTT、Glucodensity摘要）呈现，单一视图表征无法跨模态迁移；基线方法在不同模态迁移下表现不一致。需要一种能抽象出跨视图高层时序与分布结构的表征。

Method: 提出CGM-JEPA（自监督预训练，预测掩码潜在表示而非原始值）和X-CGM-JEPA（增加掩码Glucodensity跨视图目标以获取互补分布信息），在~389k条未标记CGM数据上预训练，在三个评估场景（队列泛化、静脉到CGM迁移、家庭CGM）下使用20次×2折交叉验证评估。

Result: X-CGM-JEPA在AUROC指标上于所有三个场景排名第一或第二，超过最强基线最多6.5个百分点（队列泛化）和3.6个百分点（静脉到CGM迁移，p<0.001）；模态迁移下匹配平均AUROC同时缩小族群AUROC差距25-54%；在稀疏静脉数据上分布视图提升标签感知聚类（ARI+39%，NMI+40%）。

Conclusion: 所提出的跨视图抽象表征方法能有效解决CGM多模态迁移问题，不仅提升检测性能，还改善了模型在不同族群间的公平性。

Abstract: Continuous Glucose Monitoring (CGM) can detect early metabolic subphenotypes (insulin resistance, IR; $β$-cell dysfunction), but population-scale deployment faces two coupled problems. First, the same physiological state appears through multiple views (CGM time series, venous OGTT, Glucodensity summaries), so single-view representations fail to transfer when deployment shifts the modality or setting. Second, baselines perform inconsistently across these shifts. Both problems point to one remedy: representations that abstract away from any single view to capture higher-level temporal and distributional structure. We propose CGM-JEPA, a self-supervised pretraining framework which predicts masked latent representations rather than raw values, yielding abstraction that transfers across modalities. X-CGM-JEPA adds a masked Glucodensity cross-view objective for complementary distributional information. We pretrain on $\sim$389k unlabeled CGM readings from 228 subjects and evaluate on two clinical cohorts ($N=27$ and $N=17$ public-release subsets) across three regimes (cohort generalization, venous-to-CGM transfer, home CGM) under 20-iteration $\times$ 2-fold cross-validation. X-CGM-JEPA ranks first or second on AUROC for both endpoints across all three regimes while no baseline does, exceeding the strongest baseline by up to $+6.5$ pp in cohort generalization and $+3.6$ pp in venous-to-CGM transfer (paired Wilcoxon, $p<0.001$). Under modality shift, it matches mean AUROC while redistributing toward weaker subgroups (ethnicity AUROC gap shrinks 25-54%); on sparse in-domain venous data, the distributional view lifts label-aware clustering (ARI $+39\%$, NMI $+40\%$). Code and weights: https://github.com/cruiseresearchgroup/CGM-JEPA

</details>


### [174] [Structured Analytic Coherent Point Drift for Non-Rigid Point Set Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00934)
*Wei Feng,Haiyong Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为 Analytic-CPD 的非刚性点集配准方法，用有限维结构化解析映射替代传统CPD的高斯核位移场，通过重心凝聚将后验概率转化为加权软目标点，并采用截断多元泰勒映射表示形变，配合度续策略稳定大形变配准。实验表明该方法在低误差和快速收敛方面优于标准CPD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CPD使用点索引的高斯核位移场，形变参数数量随点集规模M增大而增加（依赖M×M核系统），计算代价高且缺乏结构可解释性。本文希望用有限维解析映射替代核方法，减少参数规模并提高可解释性。

Method: 1. 保留CPD的后验对应层（高斯混合模型）；2. 通过重心凝聚恒等式将后验概率转化为加权软目标点；3. 用截断多元泰勒映射（有限维解析映射）表示形变，参数由空间维度和解析阶数控制；4. 引入度续（degree-continuation）策略，逐步激活高阶解析模式以稳定大形变配准。

Result: 在二维解析形变和三维平滑非解析形变的实验中，Analytic-CPD在代表性大形变场景下实现了比标准CPD更低的最终误差和更快的收敛速度。

Conclusion: CPD风格的概率对应与结构化解析映射相结合，为基于核的非刚性配准提供了一种更紧凑、可解释的替代方案。代码已开源。

Abstract: We introduce Analytic-CPD, a structured analytic variant of coherent point drift for non-rigid point set registration. The method retains the CPD posterior correspondence layer, but replaces the point-indexed Gaussian-kernel displacement-field M-step with a finite-dimensional structured analytic mapping estimator. Posterior probabilities from the Gaussian mixture model are condensed through a barycentric identity into weighted soft target points, converting the CPD pairwise soft-correspondence objective into a weighted analytic fitting problem. The deformation is represented by a truncated multivariate Taylor mapping of a vector-valued function, so the number of deformation parameters is controlled by the ambient dimension and the analytic order rather than by an M-by-M kernel system over the moving points. A degree-continuation strategy is further introduced to stabilize large-deformation registration by progressively activating higher-order analytic modes. Experiments on two-dimensional analytic deformations and three-dimensional smooth non-analytic deformations show that Analytic-CPD achieves lower final errors and faster convergence than standard CPD in representative large-deformation settings. The results suggest that CPD-style probabilistic correspondences and structured analytic mappings provide a compact and interpretable alternative to kernel-based non-rigid registration. Code is available at https://github.com/monge-ampere/Analytic-CPD.

</details>


### [175] [Watch Your Step: Information Injection in Diffusion Models via Shadow Timestep Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00935)
*An Huang,Junggab Son,Zuobin Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Shadow Timestep Embedding (STE)机制，发现扩散模型的时间步嵌入可作为侧信道注入恶意信息，并可用于攻击与防御目的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型中时间步嵌入（timestep embedding）虽包含丰富信息，但在安全风险和可靠溯源方面的研究严重不足，现有工作几乎未探索其在恶意信息注入方面的潜力。

Method: 通过理论分析将时间步嵌入视为位置编码映射，提出互相干性评估（mutual coherence evaluation）来解释不同时间步区间的可分离性；设计STE机制利用时间步嵌入空间编码侧信道信息，并通过调度器接口实现攻击与防御。

Result: 发现不同时间步具有不同的表示能力，可编码侧信道信息；时间步嵌入可作为强大的侧信道传递专用信息，推动对抗性生成建模的新方向。

Conclusion: 时间步嵌入是扩散模型中未被充分利用的安全风险载体，STE机制开辟了利用时间维度进行对抗性生成建模的新研究方向。

Abstract: Diffusion models have become the foundation of modern generative systems, with most research focusing primarily on improving generation efficiency and output quality. The timestep embedding component is a crucial part of the diffusion pipeline, which provides a temporal conditioning signal to the denoising network, enabling it to adapt its predictions across different noise levels throughout the process. Despite their potential to contain substantial information, timestep embeddings remain underexplored in current research, especially for security risks and reliable provenance. To fill this gap, we introduce Shadow Timestep Embedding (STE), a novel mechanism that investigates the underutilized temporal space for malicious information injection into diffusion models. In particular, when zooming in on the timestep embedding space, we find that different timesteps exhibit distinct representational capabilities that can encode side-channel information. Moreover, such encoded information can be utilized for attack and defense purposes through the scheduler interface. We present a theoretical analysis of timestep embeddings as position-encoding mappings and derive a mutual coherence evaluation that explains the separability of disjoint timestep intervals. Our findings reveal the diffusion model's timestep as a powerful side channel for carrying dedicated information, motivating new directions for adversarial generative modeling by understanding the temporal dimension.

</details>


### [176] [EventADL: Open-Box Anomaly Detection and Localization Framework for Events in Cloud-Based Service Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00936)
*Luan Pham,Victor Nicolet,Joey Dodds,Hui Guan,Daniel Kroening*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EventADL框架，首个基于事件的开放盒式异常检测与定位方法，利用事件语义模式和事件频率模式检测异常，通过干预图定位根因，在真实云系统中F1≥90%，根因定位Top-3准确率100%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异常检测与定位（ADL）主要关注指标和日志数据，事件数据尚未被探索。为填补这一空白，作者对520个真实事件进行系统分析，发现事件数据中异常及其根因的表现规律，从而设计EventADL。

Method: EventADL包含三个阶段：1) 离线训练：从历史事件数据学习事件语义模式（ESP）和事件频率模式（EFP）；2) 在线检测：识别偏离两种模式的事件流异常；3) 根因定位：构建干预图建模系统交互与检测到异常之间的关系，自动定位根因。框架支持无标签数据，输出可解释的异常及其根因。

Result: 在三个真实云服务系统和两个真实事件上的评估表明，EventADL异常检测F1分数≥90%，根因定位Top-3准确率达到100%，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: EventADL是首个基于事件数据的开放盒式ADL框架，有效利用事件数据实现高精度异常检测与根因定位，填补了该领域的研究空白。

Abstract: Anomaly detection and localization (ADL) is critical for maintaining reliability and availability in cloud systems. Recent ADL developments focus on metric and log data, leaving event data unexplored. To address this gap, we propose EventADL, the first open-box event-based ADL framework for cloud-based service systems. To motivate the design of our framework, we conduct a systematic analysis on 520 real-world incidents, and provide insights into how anomalies and their root causes manifest through event data. EventADL has three phases: offline training, online anomaly detection, and root cause localization. During the training phase, EventADL first learns Event Semantic Patterns (ESPs), which capture normal interactions between system entities using historical event data, and then learns Event Frequency Patterns (EFPs), which capture the normal frequency of known ESPs. In the online anomaly detection phase, any data in the event stream that deviates significantly from either pattern is identified as anomalous. For localization, EventADL constructs an Intervention Graph that models the relationships between recent system interactions and the detected anomalies for automatic root cause localization. The framework is designed to operate efficiently with unlabeled data and to produce interpretable anomalies with their corresponding root causes. Our evaluation on three real cloud service systems and two real-world incidents demonstrates that EventADL outperforms existing methods, achieving F1-scores of at least 90% for anomaly detection and 100% top-3 accuracy in root cause localization.

</details>


### [177] [Fusing Urban Structure and Semantics: A Conditional Diffusion Model for Cross-City OD Matrix Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00938)
*Bin Chen,Zhuoya Meng,Fang Yang,Runkang Guo,Jingtao Ding,Yin Zhang,Chuan Ai,Zhengqiu Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SEDAN模型，一种基于结构增强扩散的条件生成模型，用于通用化的通勤OD矩阵生成，通过融合语义信息与空间约束实现跨城市的鲁棒性，在真实数据上RMSE提升7.38%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通勤流建模对城市治理和交通规划至关重要，但个体意图、地理约束和社会动态的复杂交互导致通勤模式异质性高，现有生成模型难以跨城市泛化。

Method: 将城市建模为属性图（节点为区域，含人口与POI特征；边为通勤流），提出SEDAN模型：利用图-Transformer捕捉节点语义交互建模潜在出行需求，同时注入邻接矩阵（引导注意力权重）和距离矩阵（作为扩散条件）作为显式空间约束，通过融合机制联合建模语义与空间信息。

Result: 在美国多个城市真实OD数据集上，SEDAN相比最优基线WEDAN的RMSE提升7.38%，且在异构城市场景和不同结构模式下均保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 语义与空间约束的融合使SEDAN能生成行为合理且地理一致的OD矩阵，为通勤OD矩阵生成提供了有效的通用化解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate modeling of commuting flows is important for urban governance, traffic planning, and resource allocation. However, the combined influence of individual intentions, geographic constraints, and social dynamics leads to considerable heterogeneity in commuting patterns, making it difficult to develop generation models that generalize across cities. To address this issue, we propose SEDAN, a Structure-Enhanced Diffusion model conditioned on Attributed Nodes for generalizable OD matrix generation. SEDAN models a city as an attributed graph. Each region is treated as a node with demographic and point-of-interest features, and commuting flows are modeled as weighted edges. Adjacency and distance matrices are incorporated to characterize spatial structure. Based on this representation, we design a fusion mechanism within SEDAN to jointly model semantic information and spatial information. Regional semantic attributes are used to model latent travel demand through graph-transformer-based node interactions, while spatial structure is injected into the generation process as explicit constraints. The adjacency matrix guides attention weights to strengthen interactions between neighboring regions. Meanwhile, the distance matrix serves as a diffusion condition to capture spatial proximity and travel impedance. The fusion of urban semantics and spatial constraints enables SEDAN to generate OD matrices that are both behaviorally plausible and geographically coherent. Experiments on real-world OD datasets from U.S. cities show that SEDAN achieves a 7.38\% improvement in RMSE over the state-of-the-art baseline, WEDAN. It also remains robust across heterogeneous urban scenarios and varying structural patterns. Our work provides an effective and generalizable solution for commuting OD matrix generation. The code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SEDAN.

</details>


### [178] [Divergence is Uncertainty: A Closed-Form Posterior Covariance for Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00941)
*Jiarui Xing,Song Wang,Jian Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出流匹配的散度-不确定性恒等式，无需重新训练或修改架构，即可从预训练模型的散度或雅可比矩阵直接精确计算后验协方差，在MNIST上以比集成方法低约10000倍的计算量获得语义有意义的不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配生成模型中样本不确定性的量化仍是一个开放问题，现有方法要么需要重新训练辅助头、维护昂贵的集成模型，要么需要多步传播近似协方差，在训练成本、推理成本和准确性之间难以兼顾。

Method: 从理论上证明，对于任何预训练的流匹配速度场，干净数据后验协方差的迹等于速度场的散度乘以已知的时间依赖因子再加上一个常数（散度-不确定性恒等式），其矩阵形式仅依赖于速度雅可比矩阵。该恒等式是精确的、事后可计算的，无需重新训练或修改架构。

Result: 在MNIST上，该方法生成的逐像素不确定性图语义上有意义（集中在数字边界），标量不确定性分数能追踪实际预测误差，且计算量比集成或MC Dropout低约10000倍。对于MeanFlow等一步生成器，单次前向传播即可得到端到端生成不确定性。

Conclusion: 流匹配模型中不确定性量化不需要训练成本、推理成本和准确性之间的权衡，散度-不确定性恒等式提供了一种精确、高效、事后可计算的方法。

Abstract: Flow matching has become a leading framework for generative modeling, but quantifying the uncertainty of its samples remains an open problem. Existing approaches retrain the model with auxiliary variance heads, maintain costly ensembles, or propagate approximate covariance through many integration steps, trading off training cost, inference cost, or accuracy. We show that none of these trade-offs is necessary. We prove that, for any pre-trained flow matching velocity field, the trace of the posterior covariance over the clean data given the current state equals, in closed form, the divergence of the velocity field, up to a known time-dependent prefactor and an additive constant. We call this the \emph{divergence-uncertainty identity} for flow matching. The matrix-level form of the identity is similarly closed-form, depending solely on the velocity Jacobian. Because the identity is exact and post-hoc, it is computable on any pre-trained flow matching model, with no retraining and no architectural modification. For one-step generators such as MeanFlow, the same identity yields the exact end-to-end generation uncertainty in a single forward pass, eliminating the multi-step variance propagation required by all prior methods. Experiments on MNIST confirm that the resulting per-pixel uncertainty maps are semantically meaningful, concentrating on digit boundaries where inter-sample variation is highest, and that the scalar uncertainty score tracks actual prediction error, all at roughly 10,000$\times$ less total compute than ensembling or Monte Carlo dropout.

</details>


### [179] [Finite-Sample Analysis of Elimination in Active Hypothesis Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01039)
*Ziyuan Lin,Hoang Ngoc Nguyen,Jie Xu,Ivan Ruchkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种消除增强型Track-and-Stop算法，通过逐步剪枝活跃假设集并重新分配感知资源，在有限样本下实现了更短的期望停止时间，并通过理论分析和实验验证了其效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多安全关键应用需要固定置信度、有限样本下的主动假设检验问题，本文旨在研究假设消除对序贯假设检验中停止时间的影响，以提升样本效率。

Method: 提出消除增强型Track-and-Stop算法，逐步剪枝冠军特定的活跃对手集，将感知资源重新分配给存活的备选假设，并引入攻击性参数来调节消除速度与置信保证之间的权衡。

Result: 推导出期望停止时间的非渐近上界，表明消除带来的有限样本增益体现在非主导项尺度上，源于更紧的跟踪和浓缩常数；实验验证了理论预测。

Conclusion: 假设消除策略能够有效缩短停止时间，且通过攻击性参数可灵活控制消除速度与置信保证之间的平衡。

Abstract: A fixed-confidence, finite-sample problem of active hypothesis testing arises in many safety-critical applications. Situated in the context of sequential hypothesis testing, this paper studies the effect of hypothesis elimination on the stopping time. We introduce an elimination-augmented Track-and-Stop algorithm, in which champion-specific active-opponent sets are progressively pruned, and sensing effort is reallocated toward the surviving alternatives. Our analysis derives a non-asymptotic upper bound on the expected stopping time. The gain in finite-sample from elimination appears on the scale of the non-leading term, resulting from tighter tracking and concentration constants on the reduced hypothesis set. Furthermore, we introduce an aggressiveness parameter to modulate the trade-off between faster elimination and weaker confidence guarantee. An experimental study on synthetic Gaussian instances confirms the theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [180] [Learning in the Fisher Subspace: A Guided Initialization for LoRA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01046)
*Zhi-Quan Feng,Ying-Jia Lin,Hung-Yu Kao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Fisher信息的数据感知LoRA初始化方法，利用下游数据的曲率信息选择适应子空间，相比传统只基于权重重量的初始化策略，显著提升了微调性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LoRA初始化仅依赖预训练权重的固有属性（如权重大小），忽略了模型与下游数据分布的交互，导致初始化可能将容量分配给任务无关的方向，限制了微调效果。

Method: 将LoRA初始化形式化为识别参数空间中在下游数据分布下影响程度的任务，利用Fisher信息矩阵（下游数据引起的曲率信息）来衡量参数扰动对模型预测的影响，从而选择与目标任务更对齐的低秩子空间方向。

Result: 在多种任务和数据模态上的实验表明，数据感知的初始化方法在下游性能上持续且显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 数据感知的敏感性（而非仅权重重量的幅度）应主导适应子空间的选择，Fisher引导的框架提供了更原则性、任务依赖的LoRA初始化准则。

Abstract: LoRA adapts large language models (LLMs) by restricting updates to low-rank subspaces of pre-trained weights. While this substantially reduces training cost, the effectiveness of adaptation critically depends on which subspace is chosen at initialization: a poor initialization that allocates capacity to task-irrelevant directions can severely hinder downstream performance. Existing initialization strategies primarily rely on the intrinsic properties of pre-trained weights, implicitly assuming that weight geometry alone reflects task relevance. However, such criteria overlook how the model interacts with the downstream data distribution. In this work, we formulate LoRA initialization as identifying the degree of impact of directions in parameter space under the target data distribution. We argue that data-aware sensitivity, rather than weight-only magnitude, should govern the choice of adaptation subspaces. Building on this perspective, we propose a Fisher-guided framework that leverages curvature information induced by downstream data to characterize how parameter perturbations influence model predictions. This perspective yields a principled, task-dependent criterion for selecting LoRA directions that better align adaptation with the target objective. Empirical results across diverse tasks and modalities demonstrate that data-aware initialization consistently and significantly improves downstream performance over existing approaches.

</details>


### [181] [LEAP: Layer-wise Exit-Aware Pretraining for Efficient Transformer Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01058)
*Shashank Kapadia,Deep Naryan Mishra,Sujal Reddy Alugubelli,Haoan Wang,Saipraveen Vabbilisetty,Rishi Bhatia,Anupriya Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LEAP方法，解决了知识蒸馏与早退机制不兼容的问题，在不修改架构的前提下使蒸馏模型实现1.61倍实际加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发现知识蒸馏（层对齐）与基于收敛的早退机制存在系统性不兼容，导致蒸馏后的模型无法有效利用早退加速。

Method: 提出LEAP（Layer-wise Exit-Aware Pretraining），在标准蒸馏基础上增加一个辅助训练目标，约束中间层逼近最终层表示，从而统一两种范式。

Result: LEAP-MiniLM在NVIDIA L4上实现1.61倍实测加速，95%阈值下91.9%样本在7层退出，理论层数减少1.80倍，而标准蒸馏模型零加速；STS-B得分为0.760±0.006。

Conclusion: LEAP有效调和了知识蒸馏与早退机制的矛盾，为部署高效推理提供了实用指导（延迟、阈值、部署准则）。

Abstract: Layer-aligned distillation and convergence-based early exit represent two predominant computational efficiency paradigms for transformer inference; yet we establish that they exhibit systematic incompatibility under standard deployment conditions for convergence-based early exit. Distillation objectives that align intermediate student layers to teacher representations suppress the representational convergence that early-exit mechanisms exploit, rendering such mechanisms ineffective on distilled models. We introduce LEAP (Layer-wise Exit-Aware Pretraining), an auxiliary training objective that reconciles this incompatibility. LEAP requires no architectural modifications; it augments standard distillation with a single constraint ensuring intermediate layers approximate final-layer representations. LEAP-MiniLM achieves 1.61$\times$ measured wall-clock speedup (batch=1, NVIDIA L4) at $θ$=0.95, with 91.9% of samples exiting by layer 7 and 1.80$\times$ theoretical layer reduction, where standard distilled models achieve zero effective speedup. We validate across sentence similarity (STS-B: 0.760 $\pm$ 0.006) and retrieval benchmarks (BEIR), providing operational guidance including latency measurements, decision thresholds, and deployment criteria.

</details>


### [182] [GEODE: Angle-Adaptive OOD Detection with Universal Scorer Compatibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01063)
*Bruno Abrahao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GEODE方法，通过角度自适应范数损失复现异常暴露(OE)的边界校准效果，无需额外数据即可在多种OOD检测评分器上取得一致优异性能，解决了OE在距离类评分器上表现差的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OE在基于MSP的OOD检测上表现优异，但在KNN等距离类评分器上表现不佳，且需要精心筛选的辅助数据。作者希望理解OE为何有效，并设计一种无需辅助数据、在所有评分器上表现一致的OOD检测方法。

Method: 提出GEODE方法：通过角度自适应范数损失，每个样本的目标范数根据其与最近类别均值的余弦相似度动态缩放，从而保持特征几何结构；基于神经坍塌理论提供四个定理的理论保证。

Result: 在CIFAR-10上，GEODE在七种评分器上均表现优异（近OOD AUROC 89.0-92.3，远OOD达93.05）；与OE结合后在CIFAR-10上MSP 95.0/KNN 94.8，在CIFAR-100上全面超越OE；在WRN-28-10上Energy提升+4.5。

Conclusion: GEODE通过几何保持的合成校准方法，有效复现了OE的核心优势（边界校准），同时避免了OE的评分器依赖性和对辅助数据的需求，为OOD检测提供了一种更鲁棒、更通用的训练范式。

Abstract: Outlier Exposure (OE) is among the strongest training-based OOD detectors on standard benchmarks but exhibits scorer-dependent tradeoffs (e.g., strong on MSP, weak on KNN) and requires curated auxiliary data. We show why OE works: its features sit at the same geometric locus as real near-OOD data, with the boundary-adjacent quartile driving nearly all of OE's gain. OE is boundary calibration, not OOD coverage. GEODE (GEOmetry-preserving DEtection) replicates this calibration synthetically through an angle-adaptive norm loss in which targets scale per-sample with cosine similarity to the nearest class mean, preserving feature geometry where boundary structure matters. Four theorems grounded in neural collapse justify the design. GEODE works across all seven standard scorers on CIFAR-10 (near-OOD AUROC 89.0-92.3, far-OOD reaching 93.05; no catastrophic failure on any scorer). Since the OOD regime is unknown at deployment, this is the test that matters. GEODE outperforms vanilla CE at matched epoch counts. Combined with OE, GEODE reaches 95.0 MSP / 94.8 KNN on CIFAR-10 and beats OE on every scorer on CIFAR-100. The gains hold on WRN-28-10 (+4.5 Energy, 3 seeds). Unlike methods that push OOD into the classifier null space (e.g., PFS, 14.38 KNN AUROC, worse than random), GEODE's adaptive target preserves the geometry that distance-based scorers depend on.

</details>


### [183] [A dimensional R2 regression metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01066)
*Jaesung Yoo,Stefan Lemke,Jian Zhong Guo,Kanaka Rajan,Adam Hantman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Dim-R2 评分，它扩展了传统 R2 评分，解决了其维度限制（最多二维）、单标量评价不够丰富以及对低方差噪声敏感的问题，支持任意维度数据并提供多维准确度视图，在合成数据和三个多维回归数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 R2 评分在评估回归任务时存在三个关键局限：仅支持最多二维输入、将结果压缩为单一标量而忽略了预测准确度的丰富模式，以及对低方差噪声通道敏感，容易产生很大且难以解释的负值。

Method: 提出 Dim-R2（Dimensional R2 score），是对 R2 的简单扩展，可接受任意维度的数据，提供多维度的准确度视角，并降低对噪声的敏感性。

Result: 在合成正弦数据以及三个多维回归数据集上，Dim-R2 相比传统 R2 展现出了更好的可解释性和灵活性，能够突出显示回归准确度中的模式。

Conclusion: Dim-R2 作为一种可解释且灵活的评估指标，能够更好地指导回归建模，克服了传统 R2 在维度、信息量和鲁棒性方面的不足。

Abstract: R2 score is the standard metric for evaluating regression tasks, offering a normalized magnitude-agnostic measure of accuracy that captures variance. However, R2 has three key limitations: it is limited to at most two dimensional inputs, it reduces the score to a single scalar that hides rich patterns of prediction accuracy, and it is sensitive to low-variance noise channels which can yield large, uninterpretable negative values. We introduce the Dimensional R2 score (Dim-R2), a simple extension of R2 that accepts data of arbitrary dimensionality, provides a multidimensional view of accuracy, and reduces sensitivity to noise. We demonstrate its advantages on both synthetic sinusoidal data and three multidimensional regression datasets. Dim-R2 offers an interpretable and flexible metric that highlights patterns in regression accuracy, guiding regression modeling.

</details>


### [184] [Learning Discriminators for Resampling in the Ensemble Gaussian Mixture Filter through a Normalizing Flow Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01089)
*Zain Jabbar,Andrey A. Popov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种判别器引导的重采样方法（Discriminator-Informed Resampling），通过标准化流学习物理合理性判别器，改进了集成高斯混合滤波器（EnGMF），在低集成数场景下有效降低了预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准EnGMF的重采样步骤可能生成物理上不合理的后验样本，导致后续预测失去物理意义，因此需要引入物理合理性约束来提升滤波质量。

Method: 在EnGMF的后验重采样步骤中引入判别器，对候选粒子进行物理合理性评估并决定接受或拒绝；判别器通过标准化流（Normalizing Flow）学习得到。

Result: 在Ikeda映射和Lorenz '63系统上的数值实验表明，判别器引导的重采样方法在低集成数条件下始终优于标准EnGMF，误差显著降低。

Conclusion: 判别器引导的重采样有效提升了EnGMF在低集成数场景下的滤波精度，为粒子滤波的物理一致性约束提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The ensemble Gaussian mixture filter (EnGMF) is a powerful, convergent particle filter capable of medium-to-high dimensional non-linear filtering. The EnGMF relies on a resampling step that can generate physically unrealistic posterior samples, that would subsequently produce physically meaningless forecasts. This work introduces the discriminator-informed resampling procedure, that augments the posterior resampling step with a discriminator that accepts or rejects candidate particles based on their physical plausibility. In this work these discriminators are learned through a normalizing flow approach. Numerical experiments on both the Ikeda map and the Lorenz '63 system show that discriminator informed resampling procedure consistently reduces error relative to the standard EnGMF in low-ensemble regimes.

</details>


### [185] [Learning to Race in Minutes: Infoprop Dyna on the Mini Wheelbot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01096)
*Devdutt Subhasish,Henrik Hose,Sebastian Trimpe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文展示了Infoprop Dyna框架（一种基于模型的不确定性感知强化学习方法）使欠驱动独轮车机器人Mini Wheelbot仅通过11分钟的真实世界交互学习赛道竞速，无需物理仿真器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL方法依赖精心设计的物理仿真器和域随机化才能实现成功的仿真到现实迁移，作者希望绕过仿真器，使机器人直接从真实世界交互中学习。

Method: 采用Infoprop Dyna框架，这是一种最先进的不确定性感知的基于模型的强化学习方法，使机器人直接从真实世界交互中学习。

Result: Mini Wheelbot（欠驱动独轮车机器人）在11分钟的真实世界体验内学会绕赛道竞速。

Conclusion: Infoprop Dyna框架可以绕过物理仿真器的需求，使机器人直接从真实世界交互中高效学习复杂动态控制任务。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has the potential to enable robots with fast, nonlinear, and unstable dynamics to reach the limits of their performance. However, most recent advances rely on carefully designed physics-based simulators and domain randomization to achieve successful sim-to-real transfer within reasonable wall-clock time. In this work, we bypass the need for such simulators and demonstrate that Infoprop Dyna, a state-of-the-art uncertainty-aware model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) framework, can enable robots to learn directly from real-world interactions. Using Infoprop Dyna, the Mini Wheelbot, an underactuated unicycle robot, learns to race around a track within 11 minutes of real-world experience.

</details>


### [186] [Topological Neural Tangent Kernel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01110)
*Sanjukta Krishnagopal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了拓扑神经切线核（TopoNTK），一种用于单纯复形消息传递的无限宽核，能够捕捉高阶交互拓扑结构，并具有可解释的Hodge学习几何和谱偏差特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图神经切线核仅能处理二元（pairwise）结构，而许多现实关系系统包含高阶交互，需要更自然的单纯复形表示。

Method: 将Hodge分解理论引入核方法，通过下Hodge交互（共享顶点）和上Hodge交互（填充单纯形）结合，构建对高阶拓扑结构敏感的核函数。

Result: 在合成单纯形任务和DBLP高阶链接预测上验证了有效性，证明了表达性、Hodge对齐、谱学习和稳定性等性质。

Conclusion: 拓扑不仅是额外结构，还能为关系学习提供更忠实、可解释且有效的坐标系统。

Abstract: Graph neural tangent kernels give a principled infinite-width theory for graph neural networks, but inherit a basic limitation of graph models: they see only pairwise structure. Many relational systems contain higher-order interactions that are more naturally represented by simplicial complexes. We introduce the Topological Neural Tangent Kernel (TopoNTK), an infinite-width kernel for simplicial message passing on edge features. TopoNTK combines lower Hodge interactions, capturing graph-like coupling through shared vertices, with upper Hodge interactions, capturing coupling through filled simplices. This makes the kernel sensitive to topology invisible to graph kernels, allowing complexes with the same graph but different filled simplices to induce different kernels. Beyond expressivity, the Hodge structure gives the kernel an interpretable learning geometry. Edge signals decompose into gradient-like, harmonic, and local circulation components, and the spectrum of the TopoNTK determines how quickly each component is learned. This yields a topological form of spectral bias: components aligned with large-eigenvalue modes are learned quickly, while global harmonic modes, retained through the residual channel, often lie at smaller eigenvalues and are learned more slowly.
